docs/migration/DECISION-T060.md:5:Summary: Do not enable or implement the optional MigrationPlan.ainetops.io/v1alpha1 CRD at Phase 6. The deterministic library and CLI provide stable provenance annotations and reproducible outputs sufficient for audit in this phase, per plan.md section 7. No in-cluster workload is created at this stage.
